Rule codes: kind/enforcement coherence, `must_run`⇒grid allow (`must_run_grid_conflict`), condition-tree depth/size, cloud-vs-local signal hysteresis (`cloud_signal_hysteresis`, D1 - price signals compile to windows and carry no `reset_value`/`max_age_s`), catalog signal names (`unknown_signal`, from `ConsumerSignalCatalog` / `signals.ts`), target value coherence, 24:00 recurrence, the levels/D4 power-range rules. `examples/consumer-policy.*.json` (3 valid + 1 JSON-schema-invalid) are pinned by both validators + Python jsonschema. `content_hash` is derived server-side from canonical JSON (`ConsumerService.contentHash`), NOT in the document.
- **Migration `V20260810000000__consumer_profile_and_policy.sql`** (date-versioned ABOVE the highest shipped stand): `consumer_profile` (one row per entity - `entity_id` FK to `measurement_point`, control_kind/rated/levels/min/resolution/**`power_ranges_kw` JSONB**/storage_relation/grid policy/failsafe/enabled/version; a composite FK `(entity_id, tenant_id, site_id) → measurement_point(id, tenant_id, site_id)` pins tenant/site consistency - the unique key on measurement_point is added FIRST) + `consumer_policy` (versioned draft/active/retired, `UNIQUE(entity_id, version)`, ≤1 active). ENABLE+FORCE RLS + default-deny + app-role grants exactly like `flow_definition`/`measurement_point`. **`consumer_requirement_state` is DELIBERATELY NOT created** (no writer until the fulfilment path lands). The D4 disjointness + levels rules are enforced in the app validator (the FlowGraphValidator precedent), not a DB CHECK.
- **Entity catalog:** `entitytypes/catalog.json` gained **`pump`** (category consumer, controllable, non-composed, like wallbox/heating-rod/generic-load); the portal has no separate copy to sync. §16 capability rule: a consumer's requested `controlKind` is mapped onto the INTERSECTION of the device type's `default_actuate` (`continuous`/`stepped` need `setpoint_kw`; `on_off` needs `on_off`) - so `generic-load` (no setpoint) is on_off-only and a `continuous` request is **422**, never silently widened.
- **API `SiteConsumerController`** (`/api/v1/sites/{siteId}/...`, tenant-scoped like every site route - NO `@PreAuthorize`, RLS is the fence, foreign site 404, body tenant never trusted): `GET consumer-options` (capability-/context-filtered types + the signal catalog + intents + `hasStorage` + reported edge sources + defaults), `GET/POST consumers`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE consumers/{id}` (PATCH optimistic via `expectedVersion` → 409; DELETE = fachlich stilllegen, **409 while still connected** = edge_source/device bound), `GET/PUT consumers/{id}/policy` (a new DRAFT version; the document's `entity_id` is stamped from the path). `ConsumerService` creates the consumer via `EntityRegistryService.createEntity` (a proper v2 entity + registry push) then inserts the profile; a connected consumer binds `measurement_point.edge_source_id`. Routes in `openapi.yaml` (tag `consumer`). Proofs: `ConsumerPolicyValidatorTest` (shared vectors + fixtures, always runs) + `ConsumerApiTest` (Testcontainers: options filtering, draft/connected create, the §16 422, optimistic 409, DELETE-while-connected 409, policy draft versioning + German 400 on invalid, RLS 404).
- **Portal:** pure `src/consumers/` (`validate.ts` twin + `signals.ts` + `questions.ts` `consumerQuestions(ctx, draft)` the §14.4 Fragenbaum + `policy.ts` `buildPolicyDocument`/`policySentence`/`reviewFacts`; unit-tested `validate.test.ts`/`questions.test.ts`/`policy.test.ts`). The rule builder's permanent German preview + the review both derive from the SAME document, so they can never disagree. A flexible task carries the honest §14.7 note "Bei Verbindungsausfall kann diese Aufgabe entfallen." (no local deadline fallback yet). D3 (kWh goals only with a measurement channel, else the honest "Ohne Messung …" note) + D6 (Energiepräferenz is a required question at storage sites) are in `consumerQuestions`. **Surface SINCE Einheitsmodell Stufe 5a: the standalone Verbraucher PAGE is GONE.** Its rules live as cards in the „Regeln" capsule of Steuerung, its device in the Anlagen-Modell; the two drawers moved VERBATIM to `components/VerbraucherDrawers.tsx` (`VerbraucherAnlegenDrawer`/`VerbraucherRegelDrawer`) and are hosted from there. `verbraucher` is no longer an `AnlagenSub` — it is a `LEGACY_SUBS` redirect to `steuerung`, and the deep-link params (`?vorlage=`/`?verbraucher=`) travel along, so every bookmark keeps working. Details in `frontend/portal/AGENTS.md`. Copy guard (`copy.test.ts`) auto-scans the consumer files - they use only customer vocabulary. Details in `frontend/portal/AGENTS.md`.

## v1 -> v2 site migration mechanism (MIG)

The rehearsed, REVERSIBLE, per-site cutover from a v1 site to the adaptive v2 EMS - full operator procedure in [`docs/migration-v1-to-v2.md`](docs/migration-v1-to-v2.md). The live sites are read-only (no battery control), so migration = data conversion + a flag flip + the edge image; NO device-control certification is on the path, and v1 stays byte-identical throughout (everything v2 is flag/instant-gated).

- **Conversion preview (dry-run).** `GET /api/v1/admin/sites/{id}/v2-entities/preview` (`EntityRegistryService.preview`, `@Transactional(readOnly=true)`) composes EXACTLY what the idempotent `/bootstrap` would create/refresh - the pilot entities, their derived AE1 topology `roles`, capabilities/guards, the resolved `gatewayDevice`/`gatewayReason` and `alreadyConverted` - WITHOUT writing. `action` is `create`/`refresh`. Roles come from `TopologyDeriver.defaultRole` (the same truth the live topology uses), so preview never disagrees with the rendered view. Bootstrap itself never mutates v1 tables (asset/site/telemetry) - it only stamps `measurement_point.entity_type/capabilities/guard_config`. Plain PV+battery → a single `battery-hybrid` entity (PV+Speicher from the hybrid); a `grid-meter`/`producer` entity appears only when the matching measurement point exists.
- **History bridge (the trickiest piece).** `site.v2_history_cutover_at` (migration `V20260719070000`, nullable; NULL = un-migrated = byte-identical v1) splices the portal Historie read-side: `HistoryService` gives v1 (`telemetry`/`telemetry_rollup_*`) the buckets whose start is BEFORE the instant and the RECONSTRUCTED v2 era (`telemetry_v2`/`telemetry_v2_rollup_15m`) those at/after it - gap-free AND overlap-free by construction (v1 owns the straddling bucket). `HistoryRepository.v2DayBuckets`/`v2RollupBuckets`/`v2CostPerBucket` rebuild the v1 `HistoryBucketDto` shape by SUMMING per-quarter energies (dimensionally matching v1 at every range - never avg×span): pv=Σ pv_power_kw, battery=battery_power_kw (measured), grid=grid-meter power_kw (import/export split per quarter), soc=soc_pct, load=residual grid+pv−battery. A bare-hybrid site (no grid-meter entity) leaves grid/load absent in the v2 era; PV/battery/SoC still bridge. NOTHING is copied. Admin `GET/PUT/DELETE .../v2-entities/history-cutover` controls it (DELETE = the migration rollback). Set the cutover on an hour/day boundary so week/month splice cleanly.
- **The switch = `VOLTPILOT_V2_PLAN_SITES`** (optimizer env, comma-separated site UUIDs; `config.v2_plan_site_ids`): a flagged site ADDITIONALLY gets a co-optimized v2 shadow plan on `ems/{t}/{s}/{d}/v2/plan`, the v1 `/schedule` plan byte-identical. Now a passthrough on the optimizer in BOTH composes (default empty). Rollback = remove the site + `up -d optimization`.
- **AUTOMATIC on-deploy backfill (design + real-data measurements: firstmate `data/vp-v2-entity-backfill/report.md`).** After a deploy every existing plant renders the complete v2 face by itself - no operator step. `V2SiteBackfillRunner` (an `ApplicationReadyEvent` listener in `services/api`) iterates every un-marked site and calls the EXISTING `EntityRegistryService.bootstrapIfEligible` under that site's `TenantContext` (RLS path; the cross-tenant ENUMERATION uses the BYPASSRLS `adminJdbcTemplate`). Deliberately NOT a Flyway data migration: the composition rules live in Java and evolve, an applied migration is immutable + checksum-validated, SQL would be a second composition truth, and the registry push needs MQTT + TenantContext. Ops kill-switch `voltpilot.entities.backfill.enabled` (default true).
- **⚠ Das Anlagen-Modell KOMPONIERT SICH SELBST - NULL Nutzer-Schritte, weder Kunde noch Admin (Captain-Order 10.08.2026; Live-Befund „Mienbach").** Der Boot-Backfill oben ist EIN Auslöser, und bis hierher war er der einzige automatische: eine Anlage, die NACH dem letzten api-Start entstand oder ihr Gerät danach beanspruchte, bekam nie eine Komposition - der Admin-Bootstrap (`POST /admin/sites/{id}/v2-entities/bootstrap`) war der einzige andere Weg, und der Portal-Assistent rief ihn hinter einem `if (admin)` auf. Eine echte Neuanlage (Telemetrie floss, Fahrplan lief) zeigte deshalb dauerhaft „Noch keine Komponenten. Sobald Ihr Gerät sich meldet …" - ein Versprechen, das die Plattform nicht halten konnte. **`entities/EntityAutoComposer` ist jetzt die AUSLÖSE-Politik** (WAS komponiert wird, bleibt allein `bootstrapIfEligible` - eine Wahrheit, kein SQL-Zwilling), mit drei Auslösern jeweils an dem Moment, in dem die Stammdaten komponierbar WERDEN: **Geräte-Claim** (`DeviceController.claim`, direkt nach `autoLinkBatteryDevice` - erst dort gibt es ein eindeutiges Gateway, also komponiert eine neue Anlage ohne jede Wartezeit), **Speicher-Speichern** (`SiteBatteryController.saveBattery` - eine Anlage, die ihr Gerät VOR dem Speicher bekam, bliebe sonst ohne `battery-hybrid`) und der **getaktete Abgleich** (`V2SiteBackfillRunner.reconcile()`, `voltpilot.entities.backfill.interval-ms`, Vorgabe 5 min - er heilt Bestandsanlagen OHNE Deploy, ohne Re-Claim und ohne Klick). Der Portal-`if (admin)`-Bootstrap-Aufruf ist ERSATZLOS entfallen (ein zweiter Weg zu derselben Wirkung ist genau der Grund, warum ein Kunde nie eine Komposition bekam).
  - **Der zweite Kompositions-Grund in `bootstrapIfEligible` ist nicht kosmetisch:** neben „gar keine Entitäten" komponiert auch eine Anlage, deren Komposition HINTER ihren Stammdaten liegt (`battery != null && batteryHybridPointId == null`). Er keyt auf den PUNKT, nicht auf dessen Entitäts-Konfiguration - eine bewusst gelöschte komponierte Entität behält ihren Punkt und wird deshalb NIE still neu komponiert (die Rollback-Zusage hält).
  - **⚠ Warum KEIN MQTT-Herzschlag-Zuhörer** (die naheliegende Variante): ein eigener Zuhörer bräuchte ein NEUES `@ConditionalOnProperty`-Flag (wie alle Geschwister per Vorgabe aus), das im gitops-Repo nachgezogen werden muss - sonst läuft er in prod nachweislich nie (die dokumentierte OTA-Listener-Falle), also genau die Fehlerklasse, die dieser Umbau beendet; und sich an einen BESTEHENDEN Zuhörer zu hängen koppelte die Komposition an das Flag eines fremden Features. Der Takt braucht weder Broker noch neues Pflicht-Flag.
  - **⚠ Der Takt hat einen EIGENEN Schalter (`voltpilot.entities.backfill.reconcile-enabled`, Vorgabe an) UND prüft ihn im Methodenrumpf**, weil Springs `@EnableScheduling` GLOBAL ist: sobald eine fremde Konfiguration (OTA, Metriken) es einschaltet, wäre `reconcile()` auch ohne `EntitiesSchedulingConfig` getaktet - im Testlauf gegen gestoppte Testcontainer (die dokumentierte `@Scheduled`-Falle). surefire setzt ihn auf `false`; dass die AUSGELIEFERTE Vorgabe AN ist, prüft `V2SiteBackfillReconcileWiringTest` an der echten `application.yml`. `enabled` bleibt der Not-Aus für BEIDES.
  - **⚠ Nie in die Transaktion des Aufrufers:** `ensureComposed` verschluckt jede Ausnahme (ein Claim ist ein Betriebsvorgang, ein fehlendes Modell nur ein Anzeige-Mangel) und komponiert bei laufender Transaktion erst NACH dem Commit - sonst markierte ein Fehler im Modell-Aufbau die Transaktion als rollback-only und risse den Speicher-Schreibvorgang mit; der Mandant wird dabei ausdrücklich in den Nach-Commit-Lauf mitgenommen (sonst RLS default-deny).
  - **⚠ Folge für das Anlegen eines Netz-Zählers:** seit die Komposition beim Claim läuft, trägt jede verbundene Anlage eine SYNTHETISIERTE `grid-meter`-Zeile - die 0-1-Regel in `MeasurementPointController.create` hätte den Kunden danach für immer mit 409 abgewiesen. Ein ECHTER Zähler ÜBERNIMMT deshalb die komponierte Zeile (`MeasurementPointRepository.adoptComposedGridMeter`, erkannt an `device_id IS NOT NULL AND edge_source_id IS NULL`); zwei Zeilen wären keine Alternative, weil die Topologie je Rolle summiert. Eine gepinnte/kundeneigene Zeile bleibt unangetastet und bekommt weiter 409.
  - **⚠ Für TESTS:** ein `POST /devices/claim` komponiert jetzt - wer den UN-komponierten Zustand einer Anlage braucht (Vorschau-Dry-Run, ein Test, der die Entitäts-Menge selbst besitzt), setzt das Gerät per Superuser-INSERT ein statt zu claimen. So machen es `AdminApiTest.v2ConversionPreview…` und `PortalApiTest.entitySurfaceReportsUnreported…`.
  - **Beweise:** rein `EntityAutoComposerTest` (6: sofort vs. nach dem Commit, Mandant im Callback, nie werfend) + `V2SiteBackfillRunnerTest` (+4 Takt-Fälle) + `V2SiteBackfillReconcileWiringTest` (4: Spring baut Läufer+Composer trotz zweier Konstruktoren, die ausgelieferten Vorgaben, der Takt-Schalter, drei `@EnableScheduling` gemeinsam); Testcontainers `AdminApiTest.theAnlagenModellComposesItselfOnClaimAndHealsAnExistingPlantWithoutAnyHumanStep` (echte DB + Keycloak: ein KUNDE ohne Admin-Rolle claimt und liest sofort ein vollständiges Modell · der Bestandsfall heilt sich über den Lauf und GENAU EINMAL · Speicher-nach-Gerät vervollständigt · Anlage ohne Gerät bleibt still UND ungestempelt · RLS 404 für einen fremden Mandanten).
  - **THE COMPOSITION CONTRACT (measured, do not "restore the symmetry"):** battery-hybrid from the battery ASSET unchanged; **the `pv` ASSET never composes a producer** - it is the site AGGREGATE and the hybrid already measures `pv_power_kw`, so a second PV member DOUBLE-COUNTS (measured 5,85 kW read as 11,7 kW); a producer comes ONLY from a `pv-generation` measurement POINT or `adopt()`. **`grid-meter` and `house-load` are SYNTHESIZED from the gateway device** (v1 renders Netz + Haus from the gateway's own `power_kw` / `load_kw`; without an entity owning those channels both nodes vanish). Both are measure-only (`control=false`, no `actuate` - the DB CHECK enforces it), so nothing composed can reach a device. `bootstrap` and its `preview` twin must change in LOCKSTEP or the preview lies. **`house-load` is a NEW entity-type catalog entry** (category consumer, `controllable:false`, `composed:true`) - typing it `generic-load` lit up `hasControllableConsumer` on a house nobody can switch; the portal pins follow (`flows/validate.ts` composed set, `adaptive.ts` icon).
  - **The four guards:** automatic (boot); idempotent (bootstrap refreshes in place + a site with entity rows composes nothing); **a site with no unambiguous gateway is SKIPPED and deliberately NOT marked**, so it is retried on a later boot once a device is claimed (composing for a device-less plant would replace its honest onboarding guide with an empty Energiefluss); **reversible AND sticky** via the nullable `site.v2_backfilled_at` (migration `V20260722000000`) - the runner only looks at NULL markers, so deleting a site's entities is not silently re-migrated on the next restart. Re-arm one site by clearing its marker. The run writes ONLY `measurement_point` + `entity_registry_state` + the marker - never `asset`/`telemetry`/`device`.
  - **MIG-B1, the VALUE bridge (`services/timescale-writer` `ComposedEntityFanout`).** `telemetry_v2` is fed only by an edge publishing `edge/entities/{id}/telemetry`, and no shipped self-wiring flow does that - so a correctly composed site would render four nodes and four DASHES. The writer therefore mirrors each v1 sample onto the site's COMPOSED entities: battery-hybrid `soc_pct`/`pv_power_kw`/`battery_power_kw = power_kw − load_kw + pv_power_kw`, grid-meter `power_kw` (signed), house-load `power_kw ← load_kw`. **A NULL v1 channel writes NO row** (never a fabricated 0) and the derived battery power needs all three inputs. Only entities bound to the sample's own device, only the composed types (a producer is fed by its OWN source). Registry lookup cached per site (`voltpilot.entities.fanout.cache-ttl`, 60 s = the invalidation); the insert is the same guarded per-(entity, channel, time) insert, so it self-sunsets once a device publishes its own v2 telemetry. A fan-out failure is logged and NEVER wedges the v1 pipe.
  - **MIG-B2, the READ-side entity-history splice (`services/api` `EntityHistoryRepository`, audit H1 BLOCKER).** MIG-B1 only feeds `telemetry_v2` FORWARD, so on deploy day a migrated plant with 224k v1 telemetry rows had an **EMPTY Messwerte explorer at every range** (`telemetry_v2` = 0 rows) while its own Historie one tab away showed weeks of data. The fix is READ-side and additive - **nothing is copied, no migration rewrites data**: for a COMPOSED entity the pre-v2 part of the window is reconstructed from the v1 series through the SAME channel map MIG-B1 writes forward with (day = raw `telemetry` filtered on the entity's gateway `device_id` exactly like the fan-out; week/month/year = `telemetry_rollup_15m`, whose quarter ENERGIES convert back to the quarter's average power as kWh x 4 - the rollups have no device dimension, so those ranges are site-wide, which only differs on a multi-device site). **Splice instant = `max(site.v2_history_cutover_at, the entity's first v2 sample IN THE WINDOW)`** - the same mechanism/semantics as the site-level `HistoryService.splice`: v1 owns buckets STARTING before it, v2 those at/after it, so the series is gap-free AND overlap-free; no v2 row in the window at all (the deploy-day state) = the whole window renders from v1. A NULL v1 channel yields NO bucket (never a fabricated 0), and a v2-NATIVE entity (wallbox, generic Modbus) is byte-for-byte pure v2 as before. Proven on the REAL prod dump (throwaway restore, destroyed after): 307k v1 rows / 0 v2 rows, all 3 composed entities x 4 ranges return real buckets, and the spliced house-load integrates to 34,96 kWh = the site Historie's own `consumptionKwh` to the digit. Test: `PortalApiTest.entityHistorySplicesV1TelemetryBeforeTheV2EraForComposedEntities`.
  - **hasPv/hasStorage are CAPABILITY-based** (`UsageProfileService.signals`), the same rule `TopologyDeriver.defaultRole` uses - a hybrid is category `storage` yet MEASURES `pv_power_kw`, and the old category rule reported `hasPv:false` on every real plant, so Eigenverbrauch could never fire. `hasControllableConsumer` additionally requires a non-empty `actuate`.
  - **The DV money headline (captain-approved, portal `surface.ts`).** `marktvermarktung` composes **Einspeise-Erlös** (+ **Wert des Eigenverbrauchs** when a tariff is configured), with the steering delta as the ATTRIBUTION line under the revenue (`MoneyStream.attribution: 'steering'` → `erloesKomposition.steeringAttributionNote`). `savedEur` is a delta vs. an unregulated plant and already sits INSIDE the revenue - as a sibling summand it double-counts; as the only stream it understated Pilsting 301,46 € → 13,62 €. `moneyStreams()` dedupes per id (market and Eigenverbrauch now name the same streams).
  - **Proven on the captain's REAL prod dump** (throwaway pg16 + Keycloak + api, destroyed after): Auernheim + Pilsting each compose battery-hybrid + grid-meter + house-load, render the complete four-node Energiefluss with their real values (PV 5,9/59,0 · SoC 87/10 % · Haus 0,7/14,4 · Netz 0,0/44,6 kW) and the v1-identical money headline (27,04 € / 301,46 €) with the steering note; the device-less Mienbach is skipped and unmarked; a restart migrates nobody twice; deleting a site's entities restores its v1 face and the marker holds. Screenshots: `data/vp-v2-migration-build/shots/`.
- **Proofs.** `AdminApiTest.automaticBackfillComposesGridAndHouseFromTheGatewayAndIsGuardedAndReversible` (real DB: composition, no producer double-count, measure-only rows, asset untouched, device-less skip, idempotency, sticky rollback) + `V2SiteBackfillRunnerTest` (the guards, unit); `UsageProfileApiTest.hasPvComesFromTheMeasuredChannelsSoAHybridOnlyPlantCountsAsPv`; writer `ComposedEntityFanoutTest` (the channel map) + `WriterPipeTest.composedEntitiesAreFedFromTheV1SampleAndAnUnmigratedSiteIsUntouched`; portal `surface.test.ts`/`erloesKomposition.test.ts`; `AdminApiTest.v2ConversionPreviewMatchesBootstrapWithoutWritingAndCutoverIsControllable`; `PortalApiTest.migratedSiteHistoryBridgesV1AndV2ErasGapFree` (gap-free day+week across the seam + rollback); `services/optimization/tests/test_migration_dryrun.py` (engine-level: v2 plan bit-identical to v1 across all 8 golden scenarios, v1 byte-identical with/without the flag, mixed-fleet isolation) atop the solver-level golden suite; adaptive read-model by `AdminApiTest.topologyReadModelAggregates…`. Deploy readiness: `tools/deploy/verify-migration-deploy.sh` (prod compose resolves with flowc+simulation, the switch present, workflows parse - no deploy).

## Anlagen-Topologie-Read-Model (AE1): the ONE adaptive energy-flow read-model

The foundation of the adaptive EMS-UI theme (spec `data/vp-ems-replatform/adaptive-ems-ui-v1-spec.md` §10; contract **`docs/contracts/v2/topology-read-model.md`**): a site is an entity graph; each entity capability is assignable to a **role** (`pv`/`storage`/`grid`/`consumer`, extensible); roles aggregate ACROSS entities into the role-grouped hub topology the adaptive energy-flow diagram renders (portal AE2 / edge AE6, both follow-ups). A hybrid inverter maps to SEVERAL roles at once (its `pv_power_kw` → pv, `battery_power_kw`/`soc_pct` → storage); several entities aggregate into one role (Σ PV); the **maßgebliche** (`primary`) grid measurement is markable. Additive, v1 byte-identical (a registry-less/v1 site yields an empty topology).

- **THE load-bearing piece = ONE shared derivation, THREE twins pinned to ONE vector file.** `topology.Derive`/`DefaultRole` turns {resolved capabilities + live values} into role nodes + directed flows: Go `edge-app/core/internal/topology` (canonical), TS `frontend/portal/src/topology.ts`, Java `services/api …/topology/TopologyDeriver`. All three assert against **`docs/contracts/v2/topology-vectors.json`** (the jcs-vectors precedent) — Go and TS are byte-identical (canonical key order + `json.Marshal`/`JSON.stringify`), Java structurally equal. **Change the rules in all three + the vectors together.** Flow signs (from `live.ts`/`dashboard.js`): pv → in, consumer → out, grid `power_kw` + = import → in / − = export → out, battery `battery_power_kw` + = charge → out / − = discharge → in; 0.05 kW deadband; kW rounded to 3dp; an absent value is NEVER coerced to 0. Default `capability → role` mapping keys on the measure channel + entity `category` (`storage|producer|meter|consumer`; the edge's `entities.Entity.Category()` "measure-only" aliases "meter") — `soc_pct` feeds the storage node's SoC, not a flow member.
- **Cloud:** `GET /api/v1/sites/{id}/topology` (RLS, foreign 404) = `{schemaVersion, entities[], topology}` — the entity graph (per-capability resolved role+`primary`+latest `telemetry_v2` value+health) + the server-derived hub topology (`TopologyService`/`TopologyRepository`). Role ASSIGNMENT overrides live in `entity_role_assignment` (migration **`V20260719040000`**, RLS; no row = the `DefaultRole` mapping + default first-of-role primary), set via `PUT .../topology-roles`. `openapi.yaml` tag `topology`.
- **The customer CLEANUP levers on a v2 component (vp-bereinigung-ui-k3; the Pilsting incident 2026-07-29).** On a plant where EVERY reported device is already pinned - to the WRONG components - there is no "unassigned source", so PR #271's re-pin could only ever answer 409 and its purge-delete existed on the platform-admin route alone: the customer had no way to clean up at all. Two additions to `SiteEntityAdoptController` (RLS-fenced customer routes, the `SiteFlowController` pattern) close it:
  - **`POST .../v2-entities/{entityId}/edge-source` takes `swap: true`** - a target held by ANOTHER component is then EXCHANGED instead of refused, both pins moving inside ONE transaction (`EntityRegistryService.repin(..., allowSwap, reportedSourceIds)`). A client-side "release, then set" was rejected on purpose: a failure between the two calls strands BOTH components unassigned. **The holder gets this entity's previous source only when that source is STILL REPORTED**, else its pin is cleared - handing over a vanished id would just move the orphan defect. Absent/`false` keeps the documented 409, so an older client can never steal an assignment.
  - **`DELETE .../v2-entities/{entityId}`** is the customer twin of the admin `purgePoint` delete: the measurement point goes, a producer's kWp is released from the aggregate `asset.pv`, the edge source is freed and reappears as "Neues Gerät gefunden". Guarded twice: the platform-composed base components (`battery-hybrid` / `house-load`) and any component WITHOUT a device pin (a grid meter / house load composed from the plant's own master data is its Grundausstattung) are 422.
  - **`repin` now pushes the registry** (`pushRegistryBestEffort`) - it did not before, and the pin rides IN the pushed descriptor (`edge_source_id`, PR #272), so the device kept serving its per-source values against the OLD assignment while the portal showed the new one. Adopt/create/update/delete already pushed; the assertion that the composed revision changes is part of the test.
  - `EntityController`'s `EntityDto` carries the additive **`capacityKwp`**, so the portal's delete dialog names exactly which kWp leave the plant total instead of warning vaguely. Proven by `PortalApiTest.customerSwapsCrossedAssignmentsAndDeletesTheGhostComponent` (the captain's exact constellation: 409 without consent, swap-with-release, true exchange, no row minted, both 422 guards, kWp released, freed device unassigned, RLS 404). Portal side: `frontend/portal/AGENTS.md` "Die BEREINIGUNG hängt an der Komponente".
- **U2 role-assignment is now customer-writable + the adoption bridge landed (design `data/vp-ems-ui-overhaul/report.md` §3, "Rollen & Zuordnung" + "Vom Gerät gemeldet").** The role-assignment loop moved into `TopologyService.applyAssignments` shared verbatim by BOTH the admin `PUT /api/v1/admin/sites/{id}/topology-roles` and the NEW customer twin `PUT /api/v1/sites/{id}/topology-roles` (`SiteTopologyController`, NO `@PreAuthorize`, RLS-fenced via `sites.existsForCurrentTenant` — foreign site 404; the `SiteFlowController` pattern). Safe by construction: a role is presentation-level and never widens control (guards/arbitration key on capabilities, not roles). **Adoption** (`POST /api/v1/admin/sites/{id}/v2-entities/adopt`, admin-only first increment): `EntityRegistryService.adopt(siteId, sourceId, entityType, …)` turns an edge-reported source into a v2 entity in one step — a consumer type via `createEntity`, a composed producer/grid-meter as a source `measurement_point` composed from the catalog (producer kWp sums into aggregate `asset.pv` via `AssetRepository`, the retired ErzeugerSourcesPanel job). Idempotent per `measurement_point.edge_source_id` (migration `V20260720010000` + a partial-unique index; **the rule is ONE point per (site, edge source), always**: deleting a COMPOSED entity only clears its config and LEAVES the point pinned to the source, so `adopt` probes with the unfiltered `pointByEdgeSource` (never an `entity_type IS NOT NULL` filter, which missed exactly that row and made the re-adopt INSERT die on the unique index → opaque HTTP 500 on a button the portal keeps offering) and RE-COMPOSES the existing row, applying the producer kWp as a DELTA since the aggregate still carries what it contributed; the same migration adds `entity_observed_state.edge_role`/`edge_brand` so the "Vom Gerät gemeldet" list can suggest the type). The entities surface (`EntityController`) now carries `edgeSourceId` per entity + `role`/`brand`/`adoptedEntityId` per `localSetup` item. **Strategy chips**: `GET /api/v1/sites/{id}/entity-strategies` (`FlowService.entityStrategies`, keyed by entity id) = which active flows' D-13 claims touch each entity, for the "Ihre Geräte" read-only chips. Proven by `PortalApiTest.customerAssignsTopologyRolesForItsOwnSiteAndStrategiesAreScoped` + `AdminApiTest.adminAdoptsEdgeReportedSourcesIntoV2EntitiesIdempotently`.
- **Edge:** `Agent.Topology()` builds the read-model from the applied entity registry + latest per-entity readings (`topology.Resolve` defaults → `Derive`); the `/api/state` (+`/api/stream`) envelope carries an additive `topology` block (`web.TopologyController`). The scalar `pv_kw`/`load_kw`/`grid_limit_kw`/`soc_pct` fields stay for backward compat.
- **Proofs:** Go `internal/topology/topology_test.go` + `internal/web` (`TestStateEnvelopeCarriesTopology`); TS `src/topology.test.ts`; Java `TopologyDeriverTest` (pure, shared vectors) + `AdminApiTest.topologyReadModelAggregatesRolesFromV2EntitiesAndAssignmentIsSettable` (Testcontainers: pilot Deye+Fronius+grid-meter → PV aggregates hybrid+producer, Speicher = the battery, Netz = the maßgebliche meter; reassign/clear; RLS/auth/empty-site).

## Nutzungsprofil / EMS-Modus (AE7): the SECOND adaptation axis (usage profile)

The usage profile (`arbitrage | peak | private`) steers the whole portal/edge **emphasis** (money-/peak-/flow-centric); contract **`docs/contracts/v2/usage-profile.md`** (spec `data/vp-ems-replatform/adaptive-ems-ui-v1-spec.md` §2/§3). **ONE truth, captain-decided: the profile is DERIVED, not a competing concept** — it generalises `site.plant_kind` + the module surface + the wizard Nutzung step; additive, a registry-/flow-less site is byte-identical v1.

- **Pure deriver + emphasis map, twinned + vector-pinned.** `deriveDefault`/`effectiveProfile`/`emphasisFor` live in Java `services/api …/profile/UsageProfileDeriver` and TS `frontend/portal/src/usageProfile.ts`, both asserting `docs/contracts/v2/usage-profile-vectors.json` (`UsageProfileDeriverTest` ↔ `usageProfile.test.ts`, the topology-vectors precedent — change rules on both sides + vectors together). **Rules (peak beats arbitrage beats private):** a peak strategy node (`vp.strategy.peakshaving`/`.atypical-grid`) OR `site.leistungspreis_eur_kw` → peak; a market node (`vp.strategy.market`) OR `plant_kind=direktvermarktung` → arbitrage; else private. A SETTABLE override (`arbitrage`|`peak` only — **`private` is derived-only, NOT settable since vp-nacht-bezug-e7 §3.3**; `isProfile` rejects it, `UsageProfileController` 400s it) wins over the derivation. Emphasis per profile = which of money/peak/flow/devices is prominent|secondary|minimal|hidden — the ONE contract AE2/AE3/AE4/AE6 **render** (never re-decide). Edge (Go, AE6) not built here; it consults the emphasis via the cloud later.
- **Signals + surface (cloud authority).** `UsageProfileService` gathers roles from the entity registry (catalog `category`/`controllable`) + the `vp.strategy.*` nodes in the site's **ACTIVE** flows (`FlowRepository.activeDocuments`) + plant_kind/Leistungspreis. `GET /api/v1/sites/{id}/profile` = effective+derived profile, override, emphasis, signals (RLS, foreign 404, admin switcher). `site.usage_profile_override` (migration `V20260719050000`, nullable enum, additive over V2 RLS) is echoed on `SiteDto` and set/cleared via `PUT /api/v1/sites/{id}/profile` (null clears → auto-derive). openapi tag `profile`.
- **Node governance (spec §3).** The flow catalog carries a static `gated` flag per node type (`FlowCatalog.isGated`/`gatedTypes`; both `catalog.json` copies synced, sync test enforces): **gated** = market-/grid-near (`vp.strategy.market` + the two contract-reserved `vp.strategy.peakshaving`/`vp.strategy.atypical-grid`, gated strategy nodes; peak-shaving's runtime path is now BUILT — see "Peak-shaving flow node (E5a)"; atypical-grid stays reserved for E5b); **free** = device control + all data/logic/action nodes (self-consumption is base behaviour, no longer a strategy node — report vp-nacht-bezug-e7 §3.3). Per-site enablement `flow_gated_node_enablement` (migration `V20260719060000`, RLS) via admin `GET/PUT /api/v1/admin/sites/{id}/flow-node-governance`. `FlowGovernance` (pure) is the rule; `AdminFlowController.activate` refuses a flow with a gated-not-enabled node (`gated_node_not_enabled`) **before** the compiler is contacted — placeable in a draft, activatable only after enablement. **Footgun:** the pilot market node IS gated, so `FlowApiTest` enables it before its OFF-flag `activation_disabled` assertion; a new flow with a gated node needs enablement to activate.
- **Auto-start (spec §3).** `FlowTemplates` maps profile→starter (market/peakshaving on the battery, pilot chain shape; **`private` has NO starter — self-consumption is base behaviour, not a strategy node, report vp-nacht-bezug-e7 §3.3, so auto-start skips `no_template`**) + `FlowTemplateService.autoStart` (idempotent seeding hook — reuses `FlowRepository.insertDraft` + `FlowClaims`; skipped `already_has_flow`/`no_battery`/`no_template`) via admin `POST /api/v1/admin/sites/{id}/flows/auto-start`.
- **Proofs:** pure `UsageProfileDeriverTest`/`FlowGovernanceTest`/`FlowTemplatesTest` (starter validates for all 3 profiles) + `usageProfile.test.ts`; Testcontainers `UsageProfileApiTest` (derivation, override set/clear, Leistungspreis→peak, governance GET/PUT, auto-start created+idempotent, RLS 404). **Boundary: no AE2/AE3/AE4 rendering, no AE6 edge diagram, no E3b customer editor** — only the profile model + emphasis contract + governance + templates they consume.

## Flow-Editor MVP (E3a): admin-only build → validate → simulate → rollout

The first RUNTIME slice of the flow platform (contracts: `docs/contracts/v2/flow-graph.md` + `flow-artifact.md`, decisions D-11/D-13): platform-admins build typed flows in the portal, validate them against the V-1..V-8 rules, dry-run them on the existing Ersparnis-Simulation infrastructure, and activate them - the E2 flowc compiler is now WIRED (the former "Compiler folgt" stub is gone; see the "flowc-serve bridge" bullet). Deliberately WITHOUT live monitoring/SSE and WITHOUT customer access.

- **Storage + lifecycle:** `flow_definition` (api migration `V20260719000000`, RLS like measurement_point; PK `(flow_id, flow_version)`, partial unique index = at most one ACTIVE version per flow). The AUTHORITATIVE lifecycle (draft → simulated → active → retired) lives in the shared **`flows/FlowService`** (since E3b - see that section; both `AdminFlowController` and `SiteFlowController` are thin delegates)/`FlowRepository`; the document's `lifecycle` field is the contract's informative snapshot. The server STAMPS all identity (flow_id/version/site_id/tenant_id) into every stored document; saving onto a simulated/active version creates a NEW draft version (the response carries it - callers must not assume the version they PUT to). Endpoints under `/api/v1/admin/sites/{siteId}/flows` (platform-admin + X-Tenant-Id switcher over the RLS path, in `openapi.yaml`).
- **ONE catalog truth:** `services/api/src/main/resources/flowcatalog/catalog.json` (the initial nodes; port names PINNED by the contract example fixtures), served at `GET /api/v1/admin/flow-catalog`; the portal's `src/flows/catalog.json` is a SYNCED COPY guarded by `catalog.sync.test.ts` - change both together. Adding a node type is additive (palette/inspector/validation are data-driven).
- **U3 catalog additions + device-automation activation (design `data/vp-ems-ui-overhaul/report.md` §4.3, epic UO #514).** Three catalog nodes were added in lockstep to all three catalogs: **`vp.logic.and`/`.or`** (boolean combinators, two `a`/`b` bool inputs → one bool `result`, so the guided builder can wire compound conditions — V-1 allows it since each input takes exactly one edge; **the runtime combine keys on a COMPILE-TIME per-edge discriminator, not `msg.topic`** — see "flowc combinators" below), and **`vp.price.current`** (the current spot price as a `number` in ct/kWh, `group:daten`, compiles to a `vp-feed` with `feed:'price_current'` — the palette runtime feed is edge follow-up; feeds `vp.logic.threshold` directly, no price→number widening needed). **flowc `vp.schedule.window` now accepts the editor's ENUM `days`** (`alle`/`werktage`/`wochenende` → day-number array in compile) — previously flowc demanded a `0..6` array, so EVERY schedule flow (incl. the E3b Heizstab-Zeitplan) was silently un-compilable; the `scheduleDays()` helper fixes it (no committed fixture used it, so no pinned hash broke). **`FlowSimulationMapper` maps a DEVICE AUTOMATION** (a `vp.entity.control` action, no battery strategy node) **to the `standardSpeicher` baseline dry-run**, so a Wenn/Dann rule reaches `simuliert` and activates (a notify-only flow counts as an automation action too — `FlowSimulationMapper.hasAutomationAction` includes `vp.notify.push` — so it is simulable and activatable; only a BARE read with no action at all stays honestly unsupported). Two new fixtures + pinned hashes prove compile: `flow-graph.valid.compound-wallbox.json` (AND + schedule) + `flow-graph.valid.price-wallbox.json` (price condition); `pinned-compound-hash.txt`/`pinned-price-hash.txt` (self-seeded on first `node --test compile.test.js` run). Proofs: `FlowPeakShavingApiTest.deviceAutomationsBuildValidateSimulateAndActivate` (build→validate→simulate→activate, flag ON, fake flowc), `FlowGraphValidatorTest` combinator/price vectors + fixture-clean, `FlowSimulationMapperTest.deviceAutomationMapsToStandardSpeicherBaseline`, flowc `compile.test.js`/`serve.test.js` (2 fixtures + hashes), portal `guidedBuilder.test.ts` (round-trip incl. both E3b templates) + `steuerung.test.ts`. Portal detail: `frontend/portal/AGENTS.md` "U3 Steuerung area".
- **THREE catalogs must agree on the node-type SET: api (`flowcatalog/catalog.json`) ⟷ portal (`src/flows/catalog.json`) ⟷ flowc compiler (`edge-app/nodered/flowc/catalog.js` `TYPES`).** api↔portal is byte-equal (`catalog.sync.test.ts`); api↔flowc is guarded on THREE axes in `edge-app/nodered/flowc/compile.test.js` (#518 + #519 MEDIUM-6): **type-id set** equality, **per-type PORT shape** (api input names minus flowc's synthetic `trigger`, output names, and `requires_any_input` ↔ `requiresAnyInput`; the helper is itself tested against the pre-fix `vp.entity.control` shape so the guard is proven to bite), and **compiled-implementation reachability** — every `vp-feed` feed flowc can emit must exist in the palette's `FEEDS` whitelist, the axis that would have caught the dead `price_current` node. A type the EDITOR offers but flowc can't compile makes activation die with `compiler_rejected` ("unbekannter Katalogtyp …") AFTER the flow validated + simulated - the #518 bug where `vp.logic.gate` (bool→event, feeds `vp.notify.push`) was api/portal-only and `vp.logic.if` (bool→number, sets a value) was flowc-only. Both now exist in all three; `vp.notify.push`'s input is `event` (fed by the gate's rising edge) in all three. NOTE the catalogs are set-equal, NOT byte-equal: flowc's per-type PORT SHAPES can differ from api/portal (e.g. `vp.entity.control` is a single `value: number|bool` in flowc vs `value`/`setpoint`/`plan` in api/portal - pre-existing, out-of-scope drift); the guard checks type-id existence, not port structure. The notification automation (Schwellwert → Wenn/Dann-gate → Benachrichtigung) is pinned end-to-end: `flow-graph.valid.notify-threshold.json` fixture + `pinned-notify-hash.txt` (flowc `compile.test.js`/`serve.test.js`), `FlowGraphValidatorTest`/portal `validate.test.ts` (validates clean). api activation Testcontainers tests (`FlowApiTest`/`FlowPeakShavingApiTest`) FAKE the flowc transport (no Node in the JVM) - the flowc `serve.test.js` fixture IS the real sidecar compile proof.
- **Validator twins (the EdgeRef precedent):** `flows/FlowGraphValidator` (Java) and `src/flows/validate.ts` (TS) implement the same V-1..V-8 rules with shared broken-flow vectors (`FlowGraphValidatorTest` ↔ `validate.test.ts`); the contract example fixtures are executable checks on BOTH sides. D-13 claim derivation exists twice too (`FlowClaims` ↔ `model.ts deriveClaims`) with ONE structural rule: an entity-control node plan-fed by a DELEGATED strategy on the same entity derives NO own claim (else the pilot chain would V-5-conflict with itself). The server re-derives and REFUSES documents whose stored claims disagree - claims are not hand-editable.
- **Dry-run:** `FlowSimulationMapper` compiles the flow to its deterministic scenario mapping (a market or peak-shaving strategy → the "voltpilot" scenario incl. its Speicherschonung param; anything else → honest German 422; Eigenverbrauch is no longer a strategy node, report vp-nacht-bezug-e7 §3.3) and submits a normal Ersparnis-Simulation job over the site's master data; the flow-scoped poll endpoint flips draft → simulated on completion and records the summary.
- **Activation:** `FlowActivationService` consumes the `FlowCompiler` seam via ObjectProvider; the bean is now ALWAYS present (`FlowCompilerConfig` registers `FlowCompilerClient`), so `compiler_missing`/"Compiler folgt" is only reachable via the null-provider test seam. The gate is still the flag `voltpilot.flows.activation.enabled` (VOLTPILOT_FLOWS_ACTIVATION_ENABLED, default OFF - simulator-rig only; no production site is flagged), checked BEFORE compile, so a flag-off env refuses with `activation_disabled` and never contacts the sidecar. With the flag on: compile the REAL artifact via the sidecar, `FlowDeployment.requireArtifactShape` validates the manifest+bundle, then the D-11 publisher rolls it out. A sidecar outage / compiler rejection is caught BEFORE any DB write and returns `activated:false` (`compiler_unavailable`/`compiler_rejected`, German copy) - the flow stays "simuliert", never half-activated. The D-11 publisher `FlowDeploymentPublisher` (retained QoS1 `ems/{t}/{s}/{d}/v2/flows`, rides `voltpilot.provisioning.*`) + `FlowDeployment` (complete-desired-state set builder, 256/512 KiB budgets) carry the artifact's flowc `content_hash`/`min_palette_version` verbatim; activation stores the compiled artifact per version, so rollback = re-activating a previous version.
- **flowc combinators + the `vp.entity.control` input set (#519, pre-activation-flip fixes).** Three laws the compiler now holds, all proven by committed fixtures + pinned hashes (`flow-graph.valid.{guided-setpoint,market-starter,two-window-and}.json`): (1) **`vp.entity.control` mirrors the api catalog's inputs exactly** — `value: bool` (Ein/Aus), `setpoint: timeseries` (the guided "Sollwert setzen" rule wires `vp.logic.if.value` here) and `plan: plan` (the delegated strategy `wunsch` of the pilot chain / the AE7 starter templates), with the any-of rule `requiresAnyInput` instead of a hard-required `value`; compilation collapses all three onto the ONE `vp-desired` anchor. Before this every setpoint rule and every auto-started starter flow validated, simulated and then died at activation with `compiler_rejected`. (2) **D-13 plan-fed claim suppression** now exists in flowc too (`compile.js deriveClaims`, the twin of the api's `FlowClaims` / the portal's `deriveClaims`): a control node whose `plan` input is fed by a delegated strategy on the same entity derives NO own claim — otherwise the documented strategy→control chain V-5-conflicts with itself. (3) **`vp.logic.and`/`.or` key on a COMPILE-TIME per-edge discriminator**: the compiler emits a generated tag node per incoming combinator edge setting `msg._vp_src` to the graph port name, and the combinator body keys its `seen` map on that (AND = every declared port seen true). The former `msg.topic || "_"` key made two topic-less branches (two `vp.schedule.window`s, or two reads of one entity+channel) collapse into ONE slot, so a two-window AND was not degraded but WRONG — it fired during EITHER window. Changing any of this changes the artifact `content_hash` and therefore the pins: regenerate them DELIBERATELY (the tests no longer self-seed a missing pin, LOW-6).
- **flowc-serve bridge (the E2↔E3a connector, PR fm/vp2-flowc-wire).** The JVM api has NO Node runtime and MUST NOT reimplement the RFC-8785 JCS content hash, so the E2 flowc compiler runs as a tiny CLOUD-side sidecar `flowc` (`edge-app/nodered/flowc/serve.js` + `Dockerfile`, stdlib Node, `POST /compile` + `GET /health`, internal-only) - the `simulate-serve` pattern. `FlowCompilerClient` (impl of `FlowCompiler`, wired by `FlowCompilerConfig`, `voltpilot.flows.compiler.base-url`=`http://flowc:8099`) POSTs `{document}`, gets the compiled artifact back WITH flowc's own hash (200), or throws `FlowCompilerException` (422 = `compiler_rejected` carrying the sidecar's German message; transport/unexpected = `compiler_unavailable`). The `FlowCompilerHttp`/`JdkFlowCompilerHttp` seam lets tests fake the transport offline (the SimulationHttp/MastrHttp pattern). Sidecar added to BOTH composes (dev under the `flows` profile, prod as a normal internal service + published image) and the deploy build matrices; api env `VOLTPILOT_FLOWS_COMPILER_BASE_URL` in both. The sidecar is dependency-free (built from `canonicalize.js`/`catalog.js`/`compile.js`/`serve.js`); its `content_hash` comes from `compile.js` (the Go/JS JCS lockstep is untouched).
- **Portal:** Plattform nav `flows` → `pages/admin/FlowsPage` (Mandant→Anlage picker, cards + Vorlagen row incl. the pilot template) and `FlowEditorPage` (palette | hand-rolled SVG canvas | inspector, dark guard bar, live client validation, sim strip reusing SimulationRunView/ResultView). The flow-graph schema carries NO node positions (`additionalProperties:false`), so the canvas always renders the deterministic layered auto-layout (`src/flows/layout.ts`) - no drag persistence; connecting is click-output-then-input with type-compatible ports highlighted.
- **Two real bugs fixed en route:** (1) `frontend/portal/src/index.css` had an UNCLOSED brace in `.vp-modul-auto-row > svg` (module-surface PR, 2026-07-16) that silently swallowed EVERY later rule (incl. the Ersparnis-Simulation styles) - esbuild's "unbalanced {" build warning was the visible symptom; treat that warning as a hard error. (2) `useSimulationJob`'s poll effect armed only when `busy` flipped - BEFORE the start POST resolved and set `jobRef` - so with a memoized jobApi the FIRST run never polled; `status` is now deliberately an effect dependency.
- **Tests:** api `FlowGraphValidatorTest`/`FlowClaims` vectors, `FlowSimulationMapperTest`, `FlowDeploymentTest` (fixture artifacts), `FlowActivationServiceTest` (incl. sidecar-down → `compiler_unavailable`, nothing mutated), `FlowCompilerClientTest` (bridge failure mapping, offline via the HTTP seam), `FlowDeploymentPublishBrokerTest` + `FlowActivationBrokerTest` (real EMQX: activation→compiled artifact→retained deployment set validated against the contract fixture), `FlowApiTest` (Testcontainers e2e: bootstrap → pilot → validate → dry-run → activation refused by the OFF flag → versioning/tenancy/403; the flag-ON success path lives in the separate `FlowPeakShavingApiTest`, see "Peak-shaving flow node (E5a)"); the Node sidecar contract test `edge-app/nodered/flowc/serve.test.js` (`node --test`: graph fixture → artifact, hash == `pinned-hash.txt`, 422/400/health); portal `src/flows/*.test.ts` + `FlowCanvas.test.tsx`. NOTE this sandbox's Docker daemon maxes at API 1.41 - run Docker-gated api tests with `-Ddocker.api.version=1.41` locally (CI keeps the 1.44 default); the api needs JDK 21 (Homebrew `openjdk@21` is present). Renaming a `FlowApiTest` method reshuffles JUnit's default method order, so tests that create a flow on the shared BERLIN site MUST delete it (the `flowWithoutStrategy…` cleanup) or `adminBuilds…`'s `list.size()==1` flakes.

## Peak-shaving flow node (E5a): the `vp.strategy.peakshaving` runtime path

The honest remainder of E5a - the peak-shaving FUNCTION was already built (see "Peak Shaving / Lastspitzenkappung"); this makes the `vp.strategy.peakshaving` catalog node ACTIVATABLE through the flow platform, driving the SAME machinery (no new economics). `vp.strategy.atypical-grid` stays reserved for E5b (its economics are NOT built).

- **flowc compile (`edge-app/nodered/flowc/catalog.js`):** `strategyType(label, inputs)` is parameterized so each strategy declares the input set the api flow-catalog gives it (peakshaving/atypical-grid: `soc`; market: price + PV) + the delegated `wunsch` plan output; peakshaving compiles to the SAME delegation no-op as market (the co-optimizer + PS-3 edge peak guard drive execution - never re-implemented on the edge). Pinned by the new `flow-graph.valid.peakshaving-battery.json` fixture + `pinned-peakshaving-hash.txt` (compile.test.js + serve.test.js). The Go `flowdeploy` crosscheck only uses the strategy-less pv-surplus artifact, so it is untouched.
- **Activation coupling (`AdminFlowController`):** after the AE7 governance gate and before compile, `peakShavingGate` refuses (422 `peakshaving_not_configured`, German) when a peakshaving flow's site has no `leistungspreis_eur_kw` (admin optimizer-config) - never deploy a no-op strategy the co-optimizer would ignore. With governance enabled + a Leistungspreis + the activation flag on, activation compiles + deploys normally.
- **Dry-run (`FlowSimulationMapper`):** peakshaving → the co-optimized "voltpilot" scenario (folds in the Leistungspreis epigraph). atypical-grid is deliberately NOT simulable → an honest German refusal at the dry-run, so it never reaches a simulated state (and thus never activates).
- **Co-solver:** the `PeakShavingModule` already participates (registered when `leistungspreis_eur_kw` is set; golden `peak-shaving-ci.json` pins v1↔co equivalence). Proven-not-changed by `test_cooptimizer.py::test_peak_shaving_module_shaves_the_import_peak_in_the_co_optimizer`.
- **Tests:** flowc `serve.test.js`/`compile.test.js` (peakshaving fixture + pinned hash), api `FlowPeakShavingApiTest` (Testcontainers e2e: build → validate → dry-run → gated → 422-no-price → activated:true; atypical-grid refused at dry-run - a SEPARATE class from `FlowApiTest` because it runs with the activation flag ON), `FlowSimulationMapperTest` (peakshaving→voltpilot, atypical→refused), `test_cooptimizer.py`.

## Customer flow release (E3b): the Kunden-Freigabe of the flow editor

The customer-facing half of the flow platform (spec `data/vp-ems-replatform/adaptive-ems-ui-v1-spec.md` §3/§5, epic #476): a Portal-User builds/validates/simulates/activates the flows of THEIR OWN sites, on top of the E3a admin engine, WITHOUT weakening any server-side gate. The captain's framing: three tiers - Kunde selbst (frei) < VoltPilot richtet ein (vertragsnah, gated + Beratung) < System automatisch (§14a).

- **ONE lifecycle engine, two surfaces:** the whole E3a orchestration (CRUD + versioning + validate + dry-run + the AE7/peak-shaving-gated activation) was extracted verbatim into the shared **`flows/FlowService`** (a `@Service`; the DTOs `FlowVersionDto`/`FlowSummaryDto`/`SaveFlowRequest`/`ValidationResponse`/`GatedNodeDto`/`GovernanceResponse`/`GovernanceRequest` now live on it). `AdminFlowController` (`/api/v1/admin/…`, `@PreAuthorize("hasRole('platform-admin')")`) and the NEW **`SiteFlowController`** (`/api/v1/sites/{siteId}/flows…`, NO `@PreAuthorize` - the `SiteController`/`UsageProfileController` pattern: authenticated + RLS is the fence, foreign site → 404 not 403, admins reach it via the X-Tenant-Id switcher) both delegate to it - so there is ONE lifecycle truth and no forked gate. The customer routes mirror the admin ones minus the platform-admin gate; both carry the German `{message}` `@ExceptionHandler`.
- **Gates are NEVER relaxed for customers:** activation still refuses a flow carrying a GATED strategy node (`vp.strategy.market`/`.peakshaving`/`.atypical-grid`) with `gated_node_not_enabled` until a Portal-Admin enables that node type for the site - a customer CANNOT self-enable (the governance WRITE `PUT .../flow-node-governance` + the AE7 auto-start seed stay admin-only on `AdminFlowController`; the customer surface exposes governance READ-ONLY at `GET /api/v1/sites/{id}/flow-node-governance` so the editor can render a locked node). Free nodes (Eigenverbrauch strategy, device-control/data/logic/action) activate normally. Customer catalog: `GET /api/v1/flow-catalog` (authenticated, not admin-gated).
- **Lifecycle steps customers get:** full draft → validate → simulate → activate for FREE-node flows; **deactivate** their own flows (NEW `POST .../flows/{id}/deactivate` on BOTH surfaces + `FlowService.deactivate` + `FlowActivationService.deactivate` = retire the active version + re-publish the smaller deployment set, never gated by the activation flag - stopping a flow is always allowed; a flow with no active version → 409); delete; version-history read. NO governance WRITE, NO auto-start.
- **Portal reuses the E3a editor, does NOT fork it.** The editor is now driven by a site-bound **`BoundFlowApi`** (`src/flows/flowsApi.ts` `adminFlowApi(tenantId,siteId)` / `customerFlowApi(siteId)` factories - same response shapes, differing only in routes + tenant handling); `FlowEditorPage` takes `api: BoundFlowApi` (+ `canEnableGated`/`lockedHint`) instead of `tenantId`, so `pages/admin/FlowsPage` and the customer `pages/SteuerungSection` share the same palette/canvas/inspector. The palette is now governance-aware: a gated node is placeable for admins (they enable it) and for customers only once VoltPilot enabled it for the site, else it renders **locked** with the Beratung copy `EINRICHTUNG_DURCH_VOLTPILOT` (the old hardcoded Peak/atyp-NN locked pair is gone). Customer surface = the Anlage subpage `#/anlage/{id}/steuerung` (`nav.ts` sub `steuerung`, DetailCard on the Anlagen-Seite): own-flow cards + a template gallery. **Customer control templates** (pure `src/flows/customerTemplates.ts`, unit-tested `customerTemplates.test.ts` - each resolves against the site's entities + is applyDerivedClaims-stamped so it validates clean against the client validator): "Wallbox nur bei PV-Überschuss" (grid read → threshold → consumer on_off) and "Heizstab-Zeitplan" (schedule window → consumer on_off), FREE nodes only; plus the gated Marktoptimierung (pilot) shown with the Freischaltung state.
- **Honesty boundary on control (unchanged):** consumer desires flow through the built desired/arbitration path to the entity `…/command` topic; the physical go-e WRITE executor still does not exist (read-only driver). Templates that command a wallbox are buildable/activatable, and the UI shows lifecycle + whether the rollout published (poll-based; NO SSE - that is E14), never a fabricated live-ack. No per-flow ack read endpoint exists - do not invent one.
- **Tests:** api `CustomerFlowApiTest` (Testcontainers: the `demo` operator (tenant from JWT, no X-Tenant-Id) builds → validates → simulates → activates a FREE device-automation flow reaching `activation_disabled` = the gate LET IT THROUGH; a gated market flow gets `gated_node_not_enabled` for the customer, the customer cannot self-enable (admin governance PUT is 403), and once an admin enables it the SAME customer activation clears the gate; deactivate-not-active 409; foreign site 404 via RLS with `demo2`); the existing `FlowApiTest`/`FlowPeakShavingApiTest`/`UsageProfileApiTest` stay green (admin lifecycle unchanged). Portal `customerTemplates.test.ts` + the whole `src/flows/*` suite; `npm run build` green.

## Generic Modbus READ node (MB-M1): `vp.modbus.read` + `modbus-generic` entities

Phase 1 of "lease any Modbus device" (design: firstmate `data/vp-modbus-node-design/report.md`; the M2 gated WRITE + M3 polish are NOT built): a flow-editor DATA node (group `daten`, FREE/ungated, runtime-`edge`-only) reads one register of any Modbus-TCP device and emits a `number`; an optional `{entity_id, channel}` mapping records each reading as edge-entity telemetry through the UNCHANGED E1b pipeline (buffered v2 uplink → `telemetry_v2` → rollups → entity history/chart - zero new cloud plumbing).

- **Contract amendment D-15** (`docs/contracts/v2/README.md` decision log + a pointer sentence in flow-graph.md §6): the `vp.modbus.*` catalog domain MAY carry transport-level parameters - a scoped power-user escape hatch (read free, write governance-gated, outside the guard/arbitration model); the entity abstraction stays the default and only certified path.
- **All three catalogs in lockstep** (api/portal byte-equal, flowc set-equal) + the new validator param kind **`host`** (IPv4/RFC-1123 hostname, ≤253 chars) on both twins AND flowc. Config: host/port/unit_id/`register_kind` (holding=FC3|input=FC4)/**0-based** `address` (help names the 4xxxx footgun)/`data_type` u16|s16|u32|s32|float32/`word_order`/scale/offset/`min_read_interval_s` + the optional mapping; the register count is DERIVED from data_type (no free quantity field).
- **Validator rules, twinned** (Java `FlowGraphValidator.checkModbusReads` ⟷ portal `validate.ts` ⟷ flowc): mapping is both-or-neither (V-4); a mapped entity must exist + declare `measure:<channel>` AND must NOT be a COMPOSED type (battery-hybrid/producer/grid-meter) - a customer flow can never inject readings into the guard chain's inputs (V-6); two reads on one (entity, channel) per flow clash (V-5). Server-side the composed flag rides the additive `EntityCapabilities.composed` (from `EntityTypeCatalog`, wired in `FlowService.capabilityView`); the portal pins the pilot composed set.
- **flowc compiles to the DATA-ONLY palette node `vp-modbus-read`** (no generated code - the whitelisted-codegen stance holds; JCS/hash lockstep untouched). **vp-palette is 0.3.0** and the artifact's `min_palette_version` lifts with it, so an old device acks `unsupported` "benötigt Palette >= 0.3.0" (proven against the Go deployer). Pinned fixture `flow-graph.valid.modbus-read.json` + `pinned-modbus-read-hash.txt` + the committed `flowc/testdata/modbus-read.artifact.json` (crosscheck).
- **Codec `edge-app/nodered/modbus-tcp.js` generalized ADDITIVELY**: FC4 via the `fc` option on `buildReadRequest` / `expectFn` on `parseReadResponse`, plus `decodeValue`/`registerCount` (u16/s16/u32/s32/float32 × big/little WORD order; byte order within a register stays big-endian; non-finite floats → null, never NaN). The `sunspec` profile + every self-wiring consumer are byte-identical. FOOTGUN: the flow's test-read function node embeds a COPY of this file - re-run `nodered/build-flows.js` after editing it (`flows-sync.test.js` guards).
- **Palette runtime discipline** (`vp-palette/lib/modbus-conn.js`, the 2026-07-13 poll law as a library): ONE in-flight operation per (host, port) across ALL vp-modbus nodes in the runtime (module scope spans every tab), bounded drop-oldest FIFO queue, fresh socket per read, 8 s/8 s/30 s caps, never silent. `nodes/vp-modbus-read.js` is trigger-driven only (skip-if-busy + skip-if-recent per `min_read_interval_s`); the deadband gates the flow EMISSION only - entity telemetry records every successful read; a failed read emits NOTHING (status + rate-limited warn). `lib/modbus-tcp.js` is a byte-equality-guarded synced copy.
- **`modbus-generic` entity type** (`entitytypes/catalog.json`: category `meter`, `controllable:false`, measure-only failsafe, EMPTY default channels - per-entity, creator-declared; `EntityRegistryService.validatedCapabilities` accepts open `CHANNEL_RE` names, verified - no service change). The admin entity Drawer edits a CHANNEL LIST for creatable measure-only types (`parseChannelList` in portal `src/entities.ts`) instead of a rated power. `FlowSimulationMapper`: a MAPPED read counts as an automation action (a record-only flow reaches `simuliert` and activates); an unmapped bare read stays honestly refused.
- **Proofs:** flowc `compile.test.js`/`serve.test.js` (fixture + pinned hash + validator vectors), palette `test/modbus_spec.js` (in-process Modbus server: FC3/FC4, serialization, timeouts, queue, node e2e incl. entity publish on an aedes bus), Go `flowdeploy/crosscheck_test.go` (hash lockstep + palette-floor degradation) + `agent/entities_modbus_integration_test.go` (registry push → vp-modbus-read envelope → buffered v2 uplink with ORIGINAL ts → observed heartbeat), api `FlowGraphValidatorTest`/`FlowSimulationMapperTest`/`FlowPeakShavingApiTest.modbusReadAutomationRecordsIntoAGenericEntityAndActivates` (custom-channel entity → build → validate → simulate → ACTIVATE + composed-mapping refusal), portal `validate.test.ts`/`entities.test.ts`.

## Web portal (frontend/portal) - unified dashboard shell

React + Vite + TypeScript SPA. `npm run dev` (5173) / `npm run build` (tsc type-check + bundle).

**One shell for both roles** (the captain-approved redesign): a left sidebar (Übersicht / Meine Anlage(n) / Marktpreise / Prognosequalität + the admin-only "Plattform" group Mandanten/Benutzer/Geräte-Registry), a top bar (breadcrumb, tenant context chip, user menu) and one page at a time in the main area. The role never forks into a different app - Portal-Admins get *additive* nav plus the tenant **switcher** in the top bar (pick a tenant -> the customer pages render that tenant's data via `X-Tenant-Id`, see the admin section); customers see a read-only tenant badge.

- **U0 Kontotyp/Betriebsart frame (design `data/vp-ems-ui-overhaul/report.md` §2, epic UO #509 - the shell decision keys on the FRAME, not the site count).** `tenant.betriebsart` (api migration `V20260720000000`, nullable CHECK endkunde|betreiber) is the explicit override; the EFFECTIVE value resolves in ONE place - api `tenant/Betriebsart.effective(override, segment)`. **It is deliberately NOT segment-derived (pre-deploy audit HIGH-1, 2026-07-21): an unset override resolves to `null` = UNKNOWN**, because `segment` defaults to `CI` for every admin-console-provisioned tenant, so a `CI`→betreiber derivation would have silently flipped every existing single-plant customer from their cockpit into the operator Portfolio shell on the U0 deploy. Unknown falls back to the pre-U0 site-count heuristic in the portal, i.e. zero change for existing tenants; the Portfolio shell appears ONLY once an admin explicitly stores `betriebsart='betreiber'` (`BetriebsartTest`) - and is echoed on the admin tenant DTOs (`betriebsart` + `betriebsartEffective`) and on the customer login bootstrap **`GET /api/v1/tenant-context`** (`TenantContextController`, RLS-scoped app datasource: customers get exactly their tenant row, admins the switcher-selected one, admin-without-tenant = 404). Admins set/clear it via the existing `PUT /api/v1/admin/tenants/{id}` (full representation: omitted/null = Automatisch; select on the Mandanten drawer) - customers can never write it. The portal NEVER re-derives: `src/betriebsart.ts` (`isFleetShell`/`showOverviewNav`/`redirectOverviewToAnlage`, unit-tested) consumes the echoed value - *betreiber* = fleet shell from the FIRST Standort (interim landing = today's fleet Übersicht; the real Portfolio page is U5 #516), *endkunde* = cockpit, and with 2+ Anlagen the calm `FleetUebersicht` CARDS, never an operator table; `null` (no explicit override - the default for every existing tenant - older backend, or context not loaded) falls back to the v1 `sites.length >= 2` heuristic byte-identically. Admins keep always-Übersicht; the frame applies to the switcher-selected tenant. Proven by `AdminApiTest.betriebsartFrameIsAdminSetNeverSegmentDerivedAndEchoedOnTenantContext` + portal `betriebsart.test.ts`.

- **Structure:** `src/shell/AppShell.tsx` (sidebar/topbar/hamburger), `src/nav.ts` (page registry + tiny hash router: `#/geraete` etc.; keycloak-js runs with `responseMode: 'query'` so the auth redirect never clobbers the hash), `src/pages/*` (one file per page; `pages/admin/*` for the Plattform group), `src/components/*` (shared drawers/pickers), `src/format.ts`.
- **The Anlagen-IA (captain decisions 2026-07-07, Phase A of the portal restructure): ONE site = ONE Anlage, UI-hard (data model untouched).** The former Standorte/Geräte/Live-Daten/Fahrplan/Wetter/Historie menu items are RETIRED; everything moved onto **the Anlagen-Seite** `#/anlage/{siteId}` (one scrollable story: Kopf mit Status-Satz, Geld = EarningsHero, Jetzt = EnergyFlow, Fahrplan-Mini mit einem abgeleiteten deutschen Satz, Technik = Wechselrichter/Speicher/MaStR/Standort-Stammdaten, Mehr = Historie & Erlöse/Wetter) with subpages `#/anlage/{id}/live|fahrplan|messwerte|erloese|wetter`; the OLD hashes redirect there, bookmarks keep working. Single-Anlage customers land on "Meine Anlage"; fleets keep the Übersicht (card click opens the Anlagen-Seite) plus a "Meine Anlagen" list. New surfaces say "Anlage"; "Standort" survives only as the address inside the Technik section. Details + route model in `frontend/portal/AGENTS.md` (Phase B - onboarding single flow + portal-wide wording sweep - is separate work).
- **The repeatable entity pattern** (Mandanten, Benutzer; formerly the Standorte/Geräte pages, whose content now lives in the Anlagen-Seite's Technik section): list-in-card table + "＋ anlegen" opening a right **Drawer** + row click opening a detail Drawer. No always-open inline forms.
- **Money-first, deliberately calm (captain 2026-07-07: "zu viele Infos, schlicht gehalten"):** the Anlagen-Seite keeps the measured EarningsHero + ONE plain-German status sentence + the EnergyFlow centerpiece (fed by `GET /overview`); the live DEPTH (Verlauf chart, verdict tiles, window toggle) lives on the Anlage's **live subpage**. Single-Anlage customers see the Anlagen-Seite AS their Übersicht. Details in `frontend/portal/AGENTS.md`.
- **Zero-touch device claiming (Technik section + fleet Übersicht):** the add drawer takes ONLY Anlage + Edge-Referenz (no IDs, no commands - the captain explicitly dropped the connection-data panel); after claiming, the row shows "wartet auf erste Daten" and flips to **online** when telemetry arrives (`Device.lastSeenAt`, 5-min window in `api.ts` `deviceLiveStatus`; the page polls every 30 s).
- **Design system** lives in `frontend/portal/designsystem/` - the shared VoltPilot tokens (`tokens/*.css`: colors/typography/spacing/effects/fonts, cornflower-blue brand, Inter/Inter Tight), core/form components (`.jsx` + `.d.ts`), and visual guideline cards. The shell primitives are promoted there too: `components/shell/` (`NavItem`, `Drawer`, `KpiCard` + `shell.css`), and `Badge` gained the status variants `ok`/`warn`/`off` + `dot`. Import all `tokens/*.css` **and** `components/shell/shell.css` once at the app root (done in `src/main.tsx`); **new UI must build on these components/tokens**, not hand-rolled equivalents. Read each component's `.prompt.md` for its props.
- **UI quality conventions** (from the final polish pass, full findings log in [`docs/ui-quality-pass.md`](docs/ui-quality-pass.md)): icons ONLY via the design-system **`Icon`** component (`components/core/Icon.jsx`, Lucide-path stroke set) - never unicode/emoji glyphs, they render inconsistently across platforms; all user-visible numbers through `src/format.ts` (de-DE locale, non-breaking space before units, `deviceKindLabel`/`fmtCoords` for enum/coordinate display); customer-facing copy never names internal components (broker/pipeline/RLS/Keycloak/collector names); dashboard stat ink + scale come from the portal-scoped tokens `--vp-stat-ink`/`--vp-text-stat-sm` (defined in `src/index.css`, consumed by `Stat`/`KpiCard` with the original marketing values as fallback); charts use `containLabel: true` grids and width-aware layout so phones never clip axes. Inline metric help ("was bedeutet diese Kennzahl?") goes through the shared **`InfoTip`** component (`src/components/InfoTip.tsx`, uses the `info` `Icon`): a small info button whose bubble is rendered into `document.body` at `position: fixed` and viewport-clamped, so it never clips inside a card or off a phone edge (hover/focus on fine pointers, tap on coarse) - reuse it for new tooltips instead of hand-rolling one. The **Prognosequalität** page (`pages/PrognosePage.tsx`) uses it to self-explain: a "Was sehe ich hier?" intro + a live-vs-Schattenbetrieb legend, and `InfoTip`s glossing the only two metrics it actually renders - **Ø Abweichung (MAE, kW)** and **Skill** (`1 − Fehler/Baseline-Fehler`); the page deliberately does NOT show nMAE/Bias/planAccuracy, so those get no tooltip. Load-bearing framing (a real owner-confusion fix): the page always states the structure is **2 Prognosearten × (1 aktives Modell + höchstens 1 Schatten-Kandidat)** - the two kinds are named identically everywhere via `KIND_LABELS` ("Verbrauchsprognose (Last)" / "PV-Prognose (Erzeugung)"), the active section is "Aktive Modelle" with one bordered `.vp-kind-card` per kind, so "zwei aktive Prognosen" never reads as a duplication.
- **Responsive:** sidebar collapses to a hamburger slide-over below 1024px; below 720px the hero stacks, tables collapse to label/value cards (`.vp-table.responsive` + `data-label`), drawers become full-screen sheets, touch targets stay ≥44px.
- Auth via `keycloak-js` (`src/auth.ts`, silent-SSO check + PKCE); the access token is attached to API calls in `src/api.ts` (`freshToken()` refreshes it). Config via `VITE_KEYCLOAK_*` / `VITE_API_BASE` (no hardcoded hosts beyond the localhost defaults; LAN recipe in the README - the dev realm allows `http://192.168.2.77:5173` as an origin).
- **Direct-to-Keycloak login (no pre-step).** An unauthenticated visit redirects STRAIGHT to the Keycloak login (`main.tsx` calls `login()` the moment `initAuth()` resolves false); the old "Jetzt anmelden" card remains only as fallback. Three carve-outs keep it loop- and dead-end-free: (1) the **`#register` route** (`nav.ts isRegisterRoute()`, matches `#register`/`#/register`) renders the portal's own registration form WITHOUT redirecting - the "Konto erstellen" form lives in the SPA (custom form + seamless auto-login), NOT in Keycloak; the Keycloak login page links back to it via the theme script `deploy/keycloak/themes/voltpilot/login/resources/js/register-link.js` (`scripts=` in theme.properties), which derives the portal origin from the auth request's `redirect_uri` query param (already validated by Keycloak against the client config, so it needs NO env/realm config and is correct in dev AND prod) and caches it in sessionStorage because the failed-login `login-actions` URL carries no `redirect_uri`. Theme file changes reach a running dev Keycloak on restart (start-dev, caches off); prod `start` mode needs the force-recreate (gzip-cache footgun above). (2) A **loop guard** (sessionStorage `vp.auth.autoLoginRedirect`, `main.tsx`): set before the auto-redirect, cleared on an authenticated boot; a boot that finds it still set while unauthenticated (browser back from Keycloak, failed code exchange) shows the manual login card instead of redirecting again - Keycloak's own error states stay visible, never a redirect loop. (3) `authError` (Keycloak unreachable) never auto-redirects (it would land on a dead host outside the SPA). Logout → origin → unauthenticated boot → straight back to the Keycloak login, as intended; RegisterForm's "Zur Anmeldung" calls `login()` (Keycloak) instead of flipping to the in-app card, and an authenticated boot with a leftover `#register` hash renders the normal portal (`pageFromHash` maps unknown → uebersicht).
- **Auth/login recovery paths (dead-end-free login).** The login surface never bounces a user out of the SPA with no way back: (1) when `initAuth()` throws (Keycloak unreachable) `LoginScreen` renders with `authError` and its primary CTA becomes **"Erneut versuchen" → `window.location.reload()`** (NOT `login()`, which would just redirect to the same dead Keycloak host outside the SPA), and the register link is hidden (it also needs Keycloak). (2) A **stored** ROPC session (fresh registration) whose refresh fails on boot is retried once (`refreshStoredTokens`); if it still fails, `auth.ts` sets a module flag surfaced via `wasSessionExpired()` → `main.tsx` passes `sessionExpired` → the login screen shows "Ihre Sitzung ist abgelaufen, bitte erneut anmelden" instead of a blank card (gated on `!authError`, since a full outage shows the outage copy). (3) `freshToken()` throws `AuthRedirectError` (exported from `auth.ts`) after triggering the login redirect; `request()` catches it and **aborts the pending call** (returns a never-resolving promise) so no unauthenticated fetch fires and no raw "API-Fehler: 401" flashes before the redirect lands. (4) `request()` and `register()` never leak raw HTTP status/`statusText` into user copy - the fallback message is plain German, a server-provided German `message` overrides it, and the numeric status stays on `ApiError.status` for branch logic. (5) A **forgot-password** note ("Passwort vergessen? Bitte kontaktieren Sie unseren Support.") is shown - there is no self-service reset without SMTP (support recovers via the admin reset-password lever). (6) Registration on a **502/503** (Keycloak down: `RegistrationController` returns 503 for transport failures, passes real Keycloak 4xx/5xx through) maps to the outage copy ("in wenigen Minuten erneut"), distinct from the transient "gleich noch einmal" hint.
- Telemetry chart uses **ECharts** (`src/TelemetryChart.tsx`). Live channel is REST polling on load (WebSocket/SSE deferred - acceptable per the increment scope).
- **ONE "Anlage anlegen" flow (captain decision 5, 2026-07-07 - Phase B of the Anlagen-IA)**: creating an Anlage is one sequenced flow in `src/components/AnlageFlow.tsx` - 1 · Anlage (Name + keyless Open-Meteo address search `LocationSearch` + Karte + Anlagentyp; Strompreis/anzulegender Wert/Netzladen collapsed behind "Feineinstellungen (optional)"; bidding zone derived from the address country via `zoneForCountry`, no WGS84/Gebotszone jargon), 2 · Gerät (Geräte-ID claim, skippable), 3 · Speicher (optional battery data saved WITHOUT a deviceId so the claimed inverter auto-links as the controlling device). BOTH entry points host this one component: the first-run wizard (`src/Onboarding.tsx`, any customer with zero devices; ends on the first-data poll "Ihre Anlage ist verbunden", 90 s check-your-device hint) and the `AnlageAnlegenDrawer` for existing customers (ends on an honest summary naming skipped steps; the host reload is deferred to drawer close so a mid-flow reload can't unmount it). Pure flow logic (step resume, battery form parsing, `normalizeDeviceIdInput`, `DEVICE_ID_FIELD`) lives in `src/anlageFlow.ts` (unit-tested). Skippable at every step; a customer with an Anlage but no device resumes at the Gerät step, and the zero-device dashboard keeps the "Einrichtung fortsetzen" banner. A SINGLE-Anlage customer (no Übersicht, no list) gets the "Sie haben eine weitere Anlage? Anlage anlegen" line at the bottom of the Anlagen-Seite - without it the 1->2 transition had no entry point. Customer-facing copy deliberately avoids internal vocabulary (no "beanspruchen"/"Mandant <uuid>"/pipeline/collector/PT15M wording anywhere in the portal); per captain decision 3 the customer surface says "Anlage" everywhere - "Standort" survives ONLY in the address sense (map, Technik "Standort & Einstellungen", MaStR register address).
- **npm registry:** `frontend/portal/.npmrc` points at **public npm** (`registry.npmjs.org`) so the portal builds on a clean machine (mirrors the Maven-Central decision). The scaffold's lockfile had a corporate mirror baked in; it was repointed. Override `.npmrc` locally if you build behind a mirror.

## Fleet overview (adaptive Übersicht): `GET /api/v1/overview` + `site.plant_kind`

Phase 1 of the multi-site fleet overview (plan/decisions: firstmate `vp-multisite-overview-k4`; Phase 2 - the realized earnings engine - and Phase 3 - negative-price curtailment + Marktprämie - are BUILT, see their own sections below).
The Übersicht is now ADAPTIVE: `sites.length > 1` renders the fleet mode (money hero on the brand gradient, fleet status sentence, per-site cards); tapping a card drills into the single-site body with a "‹ Alle Standorte" back affordance.
Since Phase 2 the hero shows MEASURED money (single-site customers get the same hero); the ex-ante planned number lives on the Fahrplan page only.
No Ø-Preis KPI in fleet mode (captain decision - meaningless across bidding zones).

- **`site.plant_kind`** (migration `V20260706010000`; enum-like TEXT `direktvermarktung | eigenverbrauch`, default `eigenverbrauch`): steers the money WORDING per site - Direktvermarktung says "Mehrerlös / mehr verdient" (spot revenue is literal income), Eigenverbrauch says "Vorteil / gespart"; a MIXED fleet gets the neutral "Ihr VoltPilot-Vorteil" headline while each site card keeps its own wording (captain decision 1, 2026-07-06).
  Exposed on `SiteDto.plantKind`, editable through the normal create/update site paths (`CreateSiteRequest`/`UpdateSiteRequest`, validated; the portal site forms have an "Anlagentyp" select).
  Pure additive ALTER - no infra bootstrap mirror needed.
- **`GET /api/v1/overview`** (`OverviewController` + `OverviewRepository`, RLS-scoped app datasource - the aggregation deliberately has NO tenant/site predicate, RLS is the fence; admins use the `X-Tenant-Id` switcher like every customer endpoint): per site `{plantKind, deviceCount, onlineCount, waitingCount, worstStatus, lastSeenAt, live (newest telemetry row via a top-1 LATERAL per site), plannedSavingsTodayEur}` plus fleet `totals` and `dailySavings` (14 Berlin days for the hero mini chart).
  Semantics worth knowing: device liveness derives from `max(received_at)` per device (the store-and-forward rule; the 5-min window MUST stay in sync with `api.ts ONLINE_WINDOW_MS`); `worstStatus` = stale beats waiting beats online; `plannedSavingsTodayEur` is the ex-ante `HistoryRepository.savings` semantics (DISTINCT ON latest run per 15-min slot) over the Europe/Berlin day computed SERVER-side - this replaced the old client-side `toDateString()` slot summing in the Übersicht and its browser-local-timezone day boundary; null = "no plan", never a fake zero.
  **U5 portfolio additions (additive, same RLS fence):** per site `roleCounts` (Σ v2 entities per topology role, `entity_type`→catalog category→role: storage→storage/producer→pv/meter→grid/consumer→consumer, one entity counted once) + `usageProfile` (the effective AE7 profile, the SAME `UsageProfileDeriver` the profile endpoint runs, fed the site's entity mix + ACTIVE-flow strategy nodes + money master data), both derived in ONE round trip (`OverviewRepository.entityTypeCountsPerSite`/`activeFlowDocumentsPerSite`); plus fleet Σ `storageCapacityKwh`/`storagePowerKw` on `totals` (from the v1 `asset` battery rows, `storageTotals()`; null when no battery). Feed the Betreiber Portfolio page (frontend/portal AGENTS.md "U5 Betreiber PORTFOLIO shell"). Proven by `PortalApiTest.overviewCarriesPerSiteRoleCountsUsageProfileAndStorageTotals`.
- **Portal:** pure fleet logic in `src/fleet.ts` (wording per fleet composition, `composeFleetSentence` - green when all fine, ONE silent device turns it amber and NAMES it (captain decision 8), `siteLiveFresh` = online device AND fresh observation, so a replaying store-and-forward edge never renders old values as live); render-only components in `src/components/FleetOverview.tsx` (hero with ~0.8 s count-up + 14-day spark bars, status card, site cards with the `--vp-flow-*` channel hues); the adaptive split + drill-down state live in `UebersichtPage.tsx` (`FleetUebersicht` / `SingleSiteUebersicht`).
  Fleet CSS is the "Fleet overview" block in `src/index.css`; cards are 1-up phone / 2-up ≥720px / 3-up ≥1200px, hero+status side by side ≥1024px.
